#SHX annexing by CHI or Warlord
country_event = {
	id = kmt.1
	title = kmt.1.t
	desc = kmt.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = kmt.1.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				FROM = {
					divisions_in_state = { state = 608 size > 5 }
				}
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				FROM = {
					divisions_in_state = { state = 614 size > 8 }
				}
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				FROM = {
					divisions_in_state = { state = 607 size > 8 }
				}
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				is_in_faction_with = PRC
				factor = -100
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = yes }
			}
			ROOT = {
				every_unit_leader = {
					set_nationality = FROM
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.2 }
			set_country_flag = shx_sumitted
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.1.b
		ai_chance = { base = 5 }
		FROM = { 
			country_event = { 
				id = kmt.3
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.1.c
		trigger = {	#can't defy the overlord
			ROOT = {
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = FROM
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		effect_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				create_wargoal = {
					type = annex_everything
					target = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { 
			country_event = { 
				id = kmt.4
				days = 1 
			}
		}
		FROM = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = KMT_SHX_refused }}
		ROOT = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = SHX_SHX_refused }}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = refused_shx_offer
		}
	}
}

				FROM = {
					strength_ratio = {
						tag = ROOT
						ratio > 0.5
					}
				}
				add = 60
			}
			modifier = {
				FROM = {
					has_war = yes
					surrender_progress > 0.1
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		name = kmt.8.a
		FROM = { annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = no } }
		FROM = { country_event = { id = kmt.9 }}
	}

			FROM = {

					type = annex_everything
					target = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { id = kmt.10 days = 1 } }
	}
}

	id = kmt.9

	desc = kmt.9.d

					is_core_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			add_core_of = ROOT
		}
		add_political_power = 100
	}
}

#taiwan refused
country_event = {
	id = kmt.17
	title = kmt.17.t
	desc = kmt.17.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = kmt.17.a
		create_wargoal = {
			type = take_state_focus
			target = FROM
			generator = { 524 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
					ROOT = {
						is_faction_leader = no
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = ROOT
					is_in_faction_with = FROM
					is_faction_leader = yes
				}
				remove_from_faction = ROOT
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						FROM = {
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
							ROOT = {
								is_faction_leader = yes
							}
						}
					}
					remove_from_faction = FROM
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = kmt.25
	title = kmt.25.t
	desc = kmt.25.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_china_soviet_volunteers

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 1000	# they are aligned to us, what do you mean "overthrow"?
				MON = {
					OR = {
						has_completed_focus = MON_hold_plenum_of_central_committee
						has_completed_focus = MON_reach_out_to_moscow
					}
				}
			}
		}
		name = kmt.25.a
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { base = 85 }
		name = kmt.25.b
		remove_from_faction = MON
		MON = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = false_document_trusted
			}
			country_event = { id = kmt.26 }
		}
		ROOT = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = MON
				modifier = never_like_them_anyway
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		name = kmt.25.c
		remove_from_faction = MON
		MON = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = false_document_trusted
			}
			country_event = { id = kmt.26 }
		}
		ROOT = {
			set_country_flag = die_mongol_scum
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = MON
				modifier = never_like_them_anyway
			}
			declare_war_on = {
				target = MON
				type = annex_everything
			}
		}
	}
}

#Surrender when china has taken all asia land
country_event = {
	id = kmt.64
	title = kmt.64.t
	desc = kmt.64.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_nationalist_china_machinegun_firing

	trigger = {
		tag = FRA
		OR = {
			CHI = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
				has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
			}
			PRC = {
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
				has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
			}
		}
		AND = {
			728 = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = { tag = FRA }
					}
					AND = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
			}
			670 = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = { tag = FRA }
					}
					AND = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
			}
			671 = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = { tag = FRA }
					}
					AND = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
			}
			286 = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = { tag = FRA }
					}
					AND = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
			}
			741 = {
				OR = {
					NOT = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = { tag = FRA }
					}
					AND = {
						owner = { tag = FRA }
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		has_civil_war = no
		is_in_faction = no
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 20 }

	fire_only_once = yes

	#Surrender and offer territory
	option = {
		name = kmt.64.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 65
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
				has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
			}
			country_event = { id = kmt.65 }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
					has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
				}
				white_peace = FRA
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					728 = {
						owner = {
							tag = FRA
						}
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
				transfer_state = 728
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					670 = {
						owner = {
							tag = FRA
						}
						controller = {
							has_country_flag = kmt_demand_indochina_war
							has_country_flag = gzw_war_flag
						}
					}
				}
				transfer_state = 670
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					671 = {
						owner = {
							tag = FRA
						}
						controller = {

###### Trade ports and their events ######

#coup success
country_event = {
	id = kmt.146
	title = kmt.146.t
	desc = {
		text = kmt.146.d_1
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = kmt.146.d_2
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port = {
			transfer_state = event_target:STATE_OF_THE_PORT
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_pla_in_beijing

	is_triggered_only = yes


	#Option for those who was tried to get robbed- WAAR
	option = {
		name = kmt.146.a
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
		create_wargoal = {
			target = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port
			type = take_state_focus
			expire = 0
		}
	}

	#Option for those who was tried to get robbed- yes, please, take it mighty couper
	option = {
		name = kmt.146.b
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				has_war = yes
				factor = 95
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -10
	}

	#hehehe, another one into mine collection
	option = {
		name = kmt.146.c
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port
		}
	}
}

#coup failed
country_event = {
	id = kmt.147
	title = kmt.147.t
	desc = {
		text = kmt.147.d_1
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = kmt.147.d_2
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_riot_police_poland

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#we will denounce them
	option = {
		name = kmt.147.a
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
		event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.156 hours = 2 }
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port modifier = denounciation }
			event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port = {
				add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = denounciation }
			}
		}
	}

	#pff, unimportant
	option = {
		name = kmt.147.b
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_someone_trying_to_steal_trade_port_from_us
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 45
			modifier = {
				has_war = yes
				factor = 95
			}
		}
	}

	#damn, I am bad
	option = {
		name = kmt.147.c
		trigger = {
			tag = event_target:TAG_trying_to_get_the_port
		}
	}
}

#enemy close
country_event = {
	id = kmt.203
	title = kmt.203.t
	desc = kmt.203.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_japanese_transport_soldiers

	trigger = {
		tag = CHI
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = await_decision_tr
			has_country_flag = treasures_abroad
			has_country_flag = treasure_ransacked
			has_country_flag = no_transfer_tr
		}
		event_target:KMT_treasure_state = {
			OR = {
				any_neighbor_state = {
					controller = {
						has_war_with = ROOT
						OR = {
							tag = JAP
							is_puppet_of = JAP
						}
						has_government = fascism
					}
				}
				controller = {
					has_war_with = ROOT
					OR = {
						tag = JAP
						is_puppet_of = JAP
					}
					has_government = fascism
				}
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 0 }

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = await_decision_tr
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.203.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:KMT_treasure_state = {
					controller = {
						has_war_with = ROOT
						OR = {
							tag = JAP
							is_puppet_of = JAP
						}
						has_government = fascism
					}
				}
			}
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = kmt_national_treasure_ransacked
				days = 900
			}
			set_country_flag = treasure_ransacked
			else = {
				add_timed_idea = {
					idea = kmt_defend_the_treasures
					days = 365
				}
				set_country_flag = no_transfer_tr
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = await_decision_tr
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.203.b
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:KMT_treasure_state = { state = 613 }
			}
			clear_global_event_target = KMT_treasure_state
			605 = {
				save_global_event_target_as = KMT_treasure_state
				custom_effect_tooltip = KMT_will_be_destination_tt
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						event_target:KMT_treasure_state = { state = 605 }
					}
					clear_global_event_target = KMT_treasure_state
					325 = {
						save_global_event_target_as = KMT_treasure_state
						custom_effect_tooltip = KMT_will_be_destination_tt
					}
					else = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								event_target:KMT_treasure_state = { state = 325 }
							}
							clear_global_event_target = KMT_treasure_state
							add_timed_idea = {
								idea = KMT_treasure_abroad_idea
								days = 365
							}
							set_country_flag = treasures_abroad
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = await_decision_tr
	}
}

	desc = kmt.217.d

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = CHI
		has_idea = KMT_retreat_to_taiwan_idea
		num_of_controlled_states < 2
		524 = {
			owner = { tag = CHI }
			controller = { tag = CHI }
		}
		has_war_with = PRC
	}

	immediate = {
		PRC = {
			white_peace = CHI
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					owner =  { tag = CHI }
					NOT = {
						state = 524
					}
				}
				PRC = { transfer_state = PREV }
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_claimed_by = CHI
					is_core_of = PRC
				}
				CHI = {
					add_state_core = PREV
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = KMT_harsh_takeover
			remove_ideas = KMT_jinyuanquan_inflation
			remove_ideas = PRC_land_reform_stimulated_our_farmers
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.217.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			remove_ideas = KMT_harsh_takeover
			remove_ideas = KMT_jinyuanquan_inflation
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = KMT_retreat_to_taiwan_idea
			}
			remove_ideas = KMT_retreat_to_taiwan_idea
		}
	}
}

				factor = 0
			}

				target = ROOT

		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = FROM
			modifier = KMT_assist_in_war
		}
	}


	option = {

#doolittle raid - vladivostok
country_event = {
	id = kmt.292
	title = kmt.292.t
	desc = kmt.292.d

	picture = GFX_report_event_001

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #historical
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				NOT = {
					has_government = USA
				}
				add = 25
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.2
				is_in_faction_with = USA
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			OR = { #cant detain if at war with JAP unless also at war with USA
				NOT = {
					has_war_with = JAP
				}
				has_war_with = USA
			}
		}
		name = kmt.292.a
		USA = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.295 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						NOT = {
							tag = SOV
						}
					}
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = ROOT
					modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_detained
				}
				else = {
					add_opinion_modifier = {
						target = ROOT
						modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_detained_SOV
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				has_government = USA
				add = 75
			}
			modifier = {
				has_war_with = USA
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				is_in_faction_with = USA
				add = 100
			}
		}
		name = kmt.292.b
		USA = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.294 }
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_help
			}
		}
		JAP = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.296 }
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_help_JAP_reaction
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				is_in_faction_with = JAP
				add = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				is_puppet_of = JAP
				add = 100
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = JAP
			}
		}
		name = kmt.292.c
		USA = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.297 }
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_given_to_JAP
			}
		}
		JAP = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.298 }
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = KMT_doolittle_pilots_help_arrest
			}
		}
	}
}

#join KMT against PRC

	title = kmt.301.t
	desc = {
		text = kmt.301.d_1
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = kmt.301.d_2
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_soldiers_fighting

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = KMT_PRC_joined
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.301.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = CHI
				enemy = PRC
			}
			CHI = {
				country_event = { id = kmt.302 }
			}
			else = {
				PRC = {
					country_event = { id = kmt.302 }
					annex_country = {
						target = ROOT
						transfer_troops = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.301.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		text = kmt.340.d_fight
		trigger = {
			FROM = {
				has_country_flag = CQ_send_troops
			}
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_soldiers_fighting

	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = PRC_restart_chinese_civil_war
		hidden_effect = {
			PRC = { country_event = { id = china.108 }}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = kmt.340.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = CQ_send_troops
			}
			add_manpower = -100
			army_experience = 1
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = {
						is_in_faction_with = ROOT
					}
				}
				remove_from_faction = FROM
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = FROM
					}
				}
				declare_war_on = {
					target = FROM
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		set_country_flag = PRC_stall_time
	}

			base = 9
		}
		name = kmt.344.a
		if = {

				is_in_faction_with = FROM

		}

		}
		name = kmt.344.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = KMT_seek_cob
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				PRC = {
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				create_faction = KMT_united_chinese_gov
				add_to_faction = ROOT

					id = "PRC"

						}

					}
				}
			}
		}

		}
	}
}

#yangtze partition
country_event = {
	id = kmt.348
	title = kmt.348.t
	desc =  kmt.348.d

	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_soldiers_running

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 2 }

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = CHI
		NOT = {
			any_country = {
				NOT = {
					tag = PRC
				}
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		has_war_with = PRC
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				any_state = {
					region = 165
					controller = { tag = PRC }
				}
				OR = {
					613 = { controller = { tag = PRC }}
					596 = { controller = { tag = PRC }}
					595 = { controller = { tag = PRC }}
					600 = { controller = { tag = PRC }}
				}
			}
		}
		count_triggers = {
			amount = 3
			AND = {
				owns_state = 598
				NOT = {
					has_full_control_of_state = 598
				}
			}
			AND = {
				owns_state = 606
				NOT = {
					has_full_control_of_state = 606
				}
			}
			AND = {
				owns_state = 620
				NOT = {
					has_full_control_of_state = 620
				}
			}
			AND = {
				owns_state = 605
				NOT = {
					has_full_control_of_state = 605
				}
			}
			AND = {
				owns_state = 601
				NOT = {
					has_full_control_of_state = 601
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.348.a
		PRC = {
			country_event = { id = kmt.212 hours = 5 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = needs_approval
			effect_tooltip = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							region = 145
							region = 144
							region = 143
							region = 155
							AND = {
								region = 164
								OR = {
									state = 620
									state = 607
									state = 606
									state = 598
								}
							}
						}
						owner = {
							OR = {
								tag = ROOT
								is_puppet_of = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					PRC = {
						transfer_state = PREV
					}
				}
				white_peace = ROOT
				diplomatic_relation = {
					country = ROOT
					relation = non_aggression_pact
					active = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = kmt.347.b
	}
}

	desc = kmt.357.d

			}

					tag = KOR

					id = "KOR"
					value = 50
				}
				add_ai_strategy = {
					type = alliance
					id = "KOR"
					value = 50
				}
			}
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = ROOT
			modifier = CHI_KR_fd
		}
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = befriend
			id = "CHI"
			value = 100
		}
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = alliance

		}

country_event = {
	id = chi_jap.1
	title = chi_jap.1.t
	desc = chi_jap.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_asian_politicians

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	immediate = {
		create_faction = PRC_kang_ri_jiu_wang
	}
	
	option = {
		name = chi_jap.1.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				country_exists = SHX
				country_exists = GXC
				country_exists = XSM
				country_exists = YUN
				country_exists = SIK
				country_exists = SIC
				country_exists = XIC
				country_exists = GDC
				country_exists = HBC
				country_exists = SND
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { 
			base = 100
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			every_other_country = {
				limit = {
					exists = yes
					OR = {
						tag = XSM
						tag = SHX

#progress - 25%
country_event = {
	id = lti.7
	title = lti.6.t
	desc = lti.7.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_soldiers_fighting

	trigger = {
		PRC = {
			OR = {
				focus_progress = {
					focus = PRC_front_line_behind_enemy_line
					progress > 0.25
				}
				has_completed_focus = PRC_front_line_behind_enemy_line
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_offensive_war_with = CHI
			has_offensive_war_with = PRC
		}
		any_owned_state = {
			is_core_of = PRC
		}
		capital_scope = {
			is_on_continent = asia
		}
		has_idea = PRC_enemy_partisan_1
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 0 }
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = PRC_enemy_partisan_1
				add_idea = PRC_enemy_partisan_2
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = lti.7.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = PRC_enemy_partisan_1
				add_idea = PRC_enemy_partisan_2
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = PRC
							is_core_of = CHI
						}
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						industrial_complex > 0
						NOT = {
							state = 524
						}
					}
					damage_building = {
						type = industrial_complex
						damage = 1
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = PRC
							is_core_of = CHI
						}
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						arms_factory > 0
						NOT = {
							state = 524
						}
					}
					damage_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						damage = 1
					}
				}
			}
			20 = {
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = PRC
							is_core_of = CHI
						}
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						dockyard > 0
						NOT = {
							state = 524
						}
					}
					damage_building = {
						type = dockyard
						damage = 1
					}
				}
			}
			40 = {
				random_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = PRC
							is_core_of = CHI
						}
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						infrastructure > 0
						NOT = {
							state = 524
						}
					}
					damage_building = {
						type = infrastructure
						damage = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = KMT_event.2
	title = KMT_event.2.t
	desc = KMT_event.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_tur_machine_gunners
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	#ACCEPT
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.2.a
		overlord = {
			end_puppet = ROOT
		}
		leave_faction = yes
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_government = communism
					has_government = democratic
				}
			}
		}
		name = KMT_event.2.b
		CCR = {
			ROOT = {
				set_variable = { ROOT_democratic_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@democratic }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_democratic_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_communism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@communism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_communism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_neutrality_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_neutrality_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT_fascism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@fascism }
				clamp_variable = {
					var = ROOT_fascism_popularity
					min = 0
					max = 100
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = democratic
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = communism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = fascism
					}
					set_country_flag = ROOT_is_fascist_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_wargoal_against = ROOT
					}
					remove_wargoal = {
					    type = all
				 	   target = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = ROOT
				autonomous_state = autonomy_dominion
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
			ROOT = {
				hidden_effect = {
					set_popularities = { #Denmark keeps it government form
						democratic = ROOT_democratic_popularity
						fascism = ROOT_fascism_popularity
						communism = ROOT_communism_popularity

						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
						}

						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_democratic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
						}
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_neutral_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
							}

						clr_country_flag = ROOT_is_communistic_flag
					}
					if = {
						limit = {

						}

	desc = KMT_event.14.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_officers
	
	is_triggered_only = yes #Triggered when Finland apply Motti tactics to a state

	
	#ACCEPT
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.14.a
		add_war_support = 0.025
		CHI = {
			create_wargoal = {
				target = ROOT
				type = annex_everything
			}
		}
		CHI = {
			country_event = CHI_warlords.19
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.14.b
		add_political_power = -50
		CHI = {
			country_event = CHI_warlords.20
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = VIN
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = ROOT
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			transfer_state_to = VIN
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				VIN = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			end_puppet = VIN
			VIN = {
				
				leave_faction = yes
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = CAM
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = ROOT
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			transfer_state_to = CAM
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				CAM = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			end_puppet = CAM
			CAM = {
				
				leave_faction = yes
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = LAO
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = ROOT
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			transfer_state_to = LAO
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				LAO = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			end_puppet = LAO
			LAO = {
				
				leave_faction = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = KMT_event.53
	title = KMT_event.53.t
	desc = KMT_event.53.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_communist_congress
	
	is_triggered_only = yes #Triggered when Finland apply Motti tactics to a state

	
	#ACCEPT
	option = {

		name = KMT_event.53.a
		set_country_flag = KMT_power_struggle_over
		IF = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = CHI_soong_qingling_pop   value = CHI_huang_qixiang_pop   compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_lkmt_pop = CHI_soong_qingling_pop }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { CHI_lkmt_pop = CHI_huang_qixiang_pop }
		}

		IF = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = CHI_lin_sen_pop   value = CHI_li_zongren_pop   compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { CHI_ckmt_pop = CHI_lin_sen_pop }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { CHI_ckmt_pop = CHI_li_zongren_pop }
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { var = CHI_ckmt_pop   value = CHI_lkmt_pop   compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = CHI_ckmt_pop   value = CHI_CKS_pop   compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = {
								ruling_only = yes
								character = CHI_chiang_kaishek
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KMT_lkmt_elections
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_soong_qingling_election_fail
				set_country_flag = KMT_huang_qixiang_election_fail
			}
			set_country_flag = KMT_elections_ckmt_vic

			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						ruling_only = yes
						character = CHI_chiang_kaishek
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_CKS_election_fail
			}
			
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = CHI_lkmt_pop   value = CHI_CKS_pop   compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					NOT = {
						has_country_leader = {
							ruling_only = yes
							character = CHI_chiang_kaishek
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = KMT_elections_lkmt_vic
			country_event = {
				id = KMT_event.55
				days = 5
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						ruling_only = yes
						character = CHI_chiang_kaishek
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = KMT_event.56
					days = 10
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KMT_lz_elections
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_li_zongren_election_fail
			}
			set_country_flag = KMT_lin_sen_election_fail
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_leader = {
						ruling_only = yes
						character = CHI_chiang_kaishek
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_CKS_election_fail
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = KMT_elections_CKS_vic
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = KMT_legalize_the_party_left
					has_country_flag = KMT_lkmt_elections
				}
				country_event = {
					id = KMT_event.54
					days = 5
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KMT_lkmt_elections
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_soong_qingling_election_fail
				set_country_flag = KMT_huang_qixiang_election_fail
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KMT_lz_elections
				}
				set_country_flag = KMT_li_zongren_election_fail
			}
			set_country_flag = KMT_lin_sen_election_fail

		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = KMT_event.60
	title = KMT_event.60.t
	desc = KMT_event.60.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_china_politicians_captured
	
	is_triggered_only = yes #Triggered when Finland apply Motti tactics to a state

	
	#ACCEPT
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.60.a
		end_puppet = FROM
	}
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.60.b
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { CHI_democratic_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@democratic }
			clamp_variable = { var = CHI_democratic_popularity   min = 0   max = 100 }
						
			set_variable = { CHI_communism_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@communism }
			clamp_variable = { var = CHI_communism_popularity   min = 0   max = 100 }
						
			set_variable = { CHI_neutrality_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
			clamp_variable = { var = CHI_neutrality_popularity   min = 0   max = 100 }
						
			set_variable = { CHI_fascism_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@fascism }
			clamp_variable = { var = CHI_fascism_popularity   min = 0   max = 100 }
						
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = democratic }
				set_country_flag = CHI_is_democratic_flag
			}
					
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = neutrality }
				set_country_flag = CHI_is_neutral_flag
			}
			
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = communism }
				set_country_flag = CHI_is_communistic_flag
			}
						
			if = { 
				limit = { has_government = fascism }
				set_country_flag = CHI_is_fascist_flag
			}
						
			if = { 
				limit = { has_wargoal_against = COV }
				remove_wargoal = { type = all   target = COV }
			}
					
			set_autonomy = {
				target = FROM
				autonomous_state = autonomy_dominion
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
					
			FROM = {
				set_popularities = {
					democratic = CHI_democratic_popularity
					fascism = CHI_fascism_popularity
					communism = CHI_communism_popularity
					neutrality = CHI_neutrality_popularity
				}
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = CHI_is_democratic_flag }
							set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic }
							clr_country_flag = CHI_is_democratic_flag
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = CHI_is_neutral_flag }
							set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
							clr_country_flag = CHI_is_neutral_flag
						}
						if = {
							limit = { has_country_flag = CHI_is_communistic_flag }
							set_politics = { ruling_party = communism }
							clr_country_flag = CHI_is_communistic_flag
						}
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = CHI_is_fascist_flag }
					set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism }
					clr_country_flag = CHI_is_fascist_flag
				}
			}
		}

	}
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.60.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

					remove_idea = KMT_harpo_3
					add_idea = KMT_harpo_4
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = KMT_harpo_2
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = KMT_harpo_2
					add_idea = KMT_harpo_3
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = KMT_harpo_1
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = KMT_harpo_1
					add_idea = KMT_harpo_2
				}
			}
			else = { add_ideas = KMT_harpo_1}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = KMT_event.79.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
		add_political_power = 10
		hidden_effect = {
			CHI = { country_event = CHI_warlords.19 }
		}
	}
}

	id = KMT_wp_event.4
	title = KMT_wp_event.4.t
	desc = KMT_wp_event.4.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_chinese_japanese_handshake
	
	is_triggered_only = yes #Triggered when Finland apply Motti tactics to a state

	
	#ACCEPT
	option = {
		name = KMT_wp_event.4.a
		add_political_power = -24
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = KMT_wp_event.4.b
		FROM = { 
			leave_faction = yes 
			clr_country_flag = CHI_new_cuf_flag_to_be_sure_its_not_already_taken_lol
		}
		IF = {
			limit = {
				RNG = {
					is_subject_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				annex_country = {
					target = RNG
					transfer_troops = yes
				}
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = FROM
						is_subject_of = FROM
					}
				}
				OR = {
					state = 591
					state = 728
					state = 326
					state = 613
					state = 524
					state = 743
					state = 745
				}
			}
			set_state_owner_to = ROOT
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = FROM
						is_ally_with = FROM
						is_subject_of = FROM
					}
				}
				OR = {
					state = 941
					state = 942
					state = 716
					state = 947
					state = 610
					state = 946
					state = 328
					state = 943
					state = 715
					state = 717
					state = 944
					state = 945
					state = 761
					state = 714
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					MAN = { 
						OR = {
							is_ally_with = FROM
							is_subject_of = FROM
							is_subject_of = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner_to = MAN
				ROOT = { puppet = MAN }
			}
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = {
					OR = {
						tag = FROM
						is_ally_with = FROM
						is_subject_of = FROM
					}
				}
				OR = {
					is_core_of = MEN
					is_claimed_by = MEN
				}
			}
			set_state_owner_to = MEN
		}
		FROM = {
			set_variable = { FROM_democratic_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@democratic }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = FROM_democratic_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { FROM_communism_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@communism }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = FROM_communism_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { FROM_neutrality_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = FROM_neutrality_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { FROM_fascism_popularity = FROM.party_popularity_100@fascism }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = FROM_fascism_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
				set_country_flag = FROM_is_democratic_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = neutrality
				}
				set_country_flag = FROM_is_neutral_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = communism
				}
				set_country_flag = FROM_is_communistic_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = fascism
				}
				set_country_flag = FROM_is_fascist_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_wargoal_against = ROOT
				}
				remove_wargoal = {
				    type = all
			 	   target = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		set_autonomy = {
			target = FROM
			autonomous_state = autonomy_puppet
			end_wars = no
			end_civil_wars = no
		}
		FROM = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_popularities = { #Denmark keeps it government form
					democratic = FROM_democratic_popularity
					fascism = FROM_fascism_popularity
					communism = FROM_communism_popularity
					neutrality = FROM_neutrality_popularity
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = FROM_is_democratic_flag
					}
					set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic }
					clr_country_flag = FROM_is_democratic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = FROM_is_neutral_flag
					}
					set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
					clr_country_flag = FROM_is_neutral_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = FROM_is_communistic_flag
						}
					set_politics = { ruling_party = communism }
					clr_country_flag = FROM_is_communistic_flag
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = FROM_is_fascist_flag
					}
					set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism }
					clr_country_flag = FROM_is_fascist_flag
				}
			}
		}
		every_enemy_country = {
			CUG = {
				add_to_war = {
					targeted_alliance = ROOT
					enemy = PREV
					hostility_reason = asked_to_join
				}
